I went from doing everything to barely being able to manage what I started, and I really need to figure out what's going on.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced periods where your mood was unusually elevated, irritable, or you felt extremely confident or energetic, not just increased drive to get things done?\n\nPatient: Yes, I did. Um, when I first started this assignment, I felt almost... electrically upbeat. Just incredibly energized and on top of the world. \n\nBut then it kind of shifted into me being weirdly irritable. Like, if anyone questioned my ideas or the plans I was making, I got really annoyed. It just felt like they were slowing down something that was obvious and important. Lately, I wake up in my temporary apartment with this heavy, hollow feeling. It's just... it feels like the day is already ruined before I even drive to the plant. I just feel so down all the time now.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced a period where your mood was elevated or irritable for at least 4 consecutive days, along with other changes like decreased need for sleep, racing thoughts, or being more talkative than usual?\n\nPatient: Yes, definitely.
